# FB2 palette directions, round 2: computed checks

Generated by `output/native-review/phase-g/scripts/palette-directions-v2.cjs` on 2026-10-13. On October 13, 2026 the owner turned down A, B and C and reopened the hues, then brought A to C back as options next to D to G. A to C are read unchanged from `directions.json` (round 1, checked there against 140 pairs) and checked here against the full 156-pair list. Research behind D to F: `mobbin-brand-research.md`. G is built from the owner's reference palette (Linen Cream, Warm Beige, Mushroom Taupe, Olive Sage, Forest Green) plus Pale Copper and the derived steps listed under it; it was revised per the owner on October 13 (linen, olive and forest lead, a copper accent), and the first version is kept below as G (v1). K keeps today's hues and spends them by the seven rules in `mobbin-colour-restraint.md` (owner, October 13). WCAG 2.x ratios from the hex values in `directions-v2.json`; the canvas reads the same numbers.

Rules (unchanged from round 1): text at least 4.5:1 (no large-text exception: DESIGN.md asks 4.5:1 for all native text); a control boundary that is the only cue, the hero button's edge and the Gagal diantar stop at least 3:1; adjacent surfaces at least 1.2:1, or a named edge token at least 1.5:1 against the outer ground; a failed photo shows no band and the scrim has no straight edge. Round 2 adds four pairs per look: charcoal on canvas, the selected/heading colour on canvas, and the page button (StickyAction) fill against the canvas and its label on the fill. The pair called "Sunrise CTA" is the hero's primary button, whatever its colour in a direction.

## Values per direction

Full theme set per theme, every nativeMood key per look, and the parts. New keys against today: `heroWarn` (the Gagal diantar stop), the hero button's fill and label, the search field and track edges, `cardEdge`, `tabEdge`, `heroEdge`, the demo strip, the failed-photo ground and the overlay ink.

### D · One green, two depths

One leaf green does the brand's work: the Siang header, the button and the selected tab. Malam is the same green at night depth (today's forest, kept). The meal card is always a plain white card with a drawn edge on a rice-white page, so it never melts into the header. Sunrise steps back to a warning colour.

Where it comes from: [HelloFresh](https://mobbin.com/screens/f504dc3e-3c55-4270-bad9-628cf4c3cbc2) (green header, warm off-white page, white cards); [Starbucks](https://mobbin.com/screens/e02f36c5-8004-4844-9e37-8d9a5310cd76) (house green deep, accent green for actions); [Too Good To Go](https://mobbin.com/screens/e2b55d00-dcd9-4742-8201-69e1bf965ef2) (one deep brand hue for every action); [Glovo (dark)](https://mobbin.com/screens/47740ec5-089f-415d-b440-d8bbb797bde2) (neutral dark, brand only where selected).

### E · Sambal on a white table

A warm chili orange becomes the only brand colour and green leaves the interface (it stays in the art). Siang wears a solid sambal header, Malam a deep kopi brown with peach markers. The page is a white table, cards a warm step down with a drawn edge, and the meal card is white. Every action is the same chili with a white label.

Where it comes from: [Gojek (GoFood)](https://mobbin.com/screens/4ec458f0-18fe-4b06-963e-a38a06899763) (white room, brand only in pills, tab and badges); [Grab](https://mobbin.com/screens/f62e8c4f-d38a-436e-98ef-c2181fef0140) (solid brand header over a white list); [DoorDash](https://mobbin.com/screens/efa74da2-de38-4b51-b8d3-c56bb4b52315) (one red for every primary action, white label); [talabat](https://mobbin.com/screens/7c91f2fa-a22c-4a44-8e86-8c1d321e45d9) (orange brand, action pill in the brand).

### F · Deep teal, tonal cards

A new deep teal replaces forest, and the meal card wears it in every look, with a white button on it. Siang has a light mint header, Malam a deep ink one. Cards are one tonal step below the page with no border, as Material 3 does, and marigold is the one warm accent (today ring, Gagal diantar).

Where it comes from: [Too Good To Go](https://mobbin.com/screens/f85e52d4-7e9f-41b4-9794-d94db8421ffc) (deep teal actions, tonal cards with no border); [sweetgreen](https://mobbin.com/screens/f77bb82b-0ccc-4660-a7bd-c6938d5e46c6) (deep green-teal for selection, tonal greige cards); [Deliveroo](https://mobbin.com/screens/fa8ec815-fc60-4287-91e7-e491e4448dc4) (bright teal for icons, a darker teal ink for text); [Starbucks](https://mobbin.com/screens/e02f36c5-8004-4844-9e37-8d9a5310cd76) (status card in the brand with a white pill on it).

### G · Linen, olive and forest

Your reference palette, revised: linen, olive and forest do the work. A linen page with lighter linen cards, tab bar and meal card (the meal card edged in olive); an olive Siang header and a forest Malam header, the same two greens at night depth in dark mode; a forest button and forest markers. Beige and taupe are only hairlines. Pale copper is the reddish accent in three places: the active tracker stop, prices and the Gagal diantar stop.

Where it comes from: Owner's reference palette (five colours supplied on 2026-10-13, plus Pale Copper); Revised per owner 2026-10-13 (main colours linen, olive and forest; much less beige and taupe; Pale Copper as a reddish accent in a few places).

Base colours: Linen Cream #EDE5D8, Olive Sage #6E7C61, Forest Green #2F4A3A, Pale Copper #DA8A67, Warm Beige #D8C8B4, Mushroom Taupe #9B8D7A.

Derived tonal steps, each added only because a pair failed with the base hexes:

- **Linen light #F8F4ED** (from Linen Cream #EDE5D8): cards, tab bar, meal card and the Siang search field in light: a linen step above the linen page (1.14:1), so cards and the tab bar take a taupe hairline (#9B8D7A, 2.59:1 on the page) and the meal card an olive edge (3.56:1 on the page).
- **Olive tint #C3CBB3** (from Olive Sage #6E7C61): the light Siang header (1.34:1 off the linen page, forest text 5.79:1), header meta on forest (5.79:1), dark muted text and the dark demo strip: olive itself carries forest text at only 2.07:1.
- **Olive deep / olive ink #4F5B45 / #3F4A35** (from Olive Sage #6E7C61): muted text (5.76:1 on linen, olive is 3.56:1), the light demo strip (linen text 5.76:1), the idle marker, track and search edge on the olive tint header (4.29:1); the ink step is the Siang header meta (5.58:1, olive deep is 4.29:1).
- **Olive light #A3AD93** (from Olive Sage #6E7C61): idle marker, track and search edge on the forest and olive-night headers (4.14:1 and 4.29:1; olive is 2.2:1 there).
- **Olive night #3B4535** (from Olive Sage #6E7C61): the dark Siang header, the olive twin of the forest Malam header at night depth (1.62:1 off the dark page).
- **Forest night / forest deep #16231C / #243A2E** (from Forest Green #2F4A3A): the dark page, Malam track, dark search field and photo ground; the deep step is the dark meal card (1.26:1 on the forest header, 1.21:1 on the olive-night one, 1.33:1 on the page, plus the olive edge).
- **Forest light #A9C4B1** (from Forest Green #2F4A3A): the dark button, selected tab label, Siang/Malam pill, today ring and active markers: forest is 1.0:1 on the forest card; this step is 5.19:1 there.
- **Copper mid / copper deep #C26F4C / #A4532F** (from Pale Copper #DA8A67): light only: the active tracker stop (3.38:1 on the linen meal card; Pale Copper is 2.45:1), and prices and the Gagal diantar stop (4.97:1, text-safe).
- **Copper light #EBA88A** (from Pale Copper #DA8A67): dark prices and the dark Gagal diantar stop: Pale Copper is 3.61:1 on the forest card, under 4.5:1 for text; this step is 4.86:1. Pale Copper itself is the dark active tracker stop (4.54:1 on the meal card).

Active tracker stop (a colour only this direction sets; checked here, outside the 156-pair count, at 3:1 against the meal card): light-siang #C26F4C on #F8F4ED 3.38:1, light-malam #C26F4C on #F8F4ED 3.38:1, dark-siang #DA8A67 on #243A2E 4.54:1, dark-malam #DA8A67 on #243A2E 4.54:1.

Accent on prices (sunrise ink in H and I, copper in G, bold ink in K; text on the plain card; outside the count, at 4.5:1): light-siang #A4532F on #F8F4ED 4.97:1, light-malam #A4532F on #F8F4ED 4.97:1, dark-siang #EBA88A on #2F4A3A 4.86:1, dark-malam #EBA88A on #2F4A3A 4.86:1.

### H · Today's palette, neutral-led

Today's hues with neutrals still doing most of the work, turned up a notch. Siang has a soft apricot header and Malam a soft sage one (a warm brown and a green in dark). The meal card is a tinted card (cream by day, pale sage at night) with an edge in the mood's deep ink. Forest marks the button, the active tab, the selected pill and the today ring; sunrise ink is the highlight on the active tracker stop and on prices.

Where it comes from: Today's palette, neutral-led (forest, sunrise, cream, charcoal and today's neutrals; revised after the owner's note).

Derived tonal steps, each added only because a pair failed with the base hexes:

- **Page neutral #ECE6DA** (from today's canvas #FDFAF3 / cream): the light page one step down, so white cards and the tab bar clear 1.2:1 without a border and the tinted headers stand off it.
- **Apricot header #F2C998** (from today's Siang header #FFEFD9 (sunrise tint)): a real Siang tint: today's #FFEFD9 would be 1.10:1 on this page; this tone is 1.24:1.
- **Sage header #C5D6BE** (from today's sage #F0F3E9 (forest tint)): the Malam twin of the apricot header at the same weight, clearing 1.2:1 on the page.
- **Apricot and sage deep #D49A5E / #8FAE96, #7E5933 / #4F6B58** (from the two header tints): the meal card's edge (1.5:1 or more on the page) and the idle marker, track and search edge (3:1 or more on the header).
- **Pale sage card #F3F6EF** (from today's sage): the Malam meal card tint, the twin of cream (#FFF7E9, today's token) by day.
- **Header meta inks #5E3F22 / #33493B** (from today's #6B4A2B / forest): secondary header text at 4.5:1 or more on the deeper header tints.
- **Dark mood headers #4A3423 / #22402F** (from apricot and forest, at dark depth): dark headers that read warm and green rather than near-black, 1.5:1 or more off the dark page.
- **Dark meal cards #2E2A25 / #232B26** (from the dark headers' hues): tinted dark cards at least 1.2:1 off both the header and the page.
- **Dark edges and markers #7A5A3E / #4F7360, #B08A66** (from the dark header hues): the dark meal card edge and the Siang idle marker at 3:1 or more.
- **Dark card, forest light #292927, #9CCBAE** (from today's dark surface / forest): kept from H (v1): dark cards clear 1.2:1, and forest keeps its roles in dark mode.

Active tracker stop (a colour only this direction sets; checked here, outside the 156-pair count, at 3:1 against the meal card): light-siang #9B4309 on #FFF7E9 6.17:1, light-malam #9B4309 on #F3F6EF 6.01:1, dark-siang #F47B2A on #2E2A25 5.23:1, dark-malam #F47B2A on #232B26 5.34:1.

Accent on prices (sunrise ink in H and I, copper in G, bold ink in K; text on the plain card; outside the count, at 4.5:1): light-siang #9B4309 on #FFFEFA 6.5:1, light-malam #9B4309 on #FFFEFA 6.5:1, dark-siang #F5C9A6 on #292927 9.57:1, dark-malam #F5C9A6 on #292927 9.57:1.

### I · No moods, light and dark

No Siang or Malam: the app follows the system's light or dark mode only. One sage header (forest's light tint), a cream meal card with an apricot edge, a neutral page and white cards. Forest is the button, the active tab, selected chips and the today ring; sunrise ink marks the active tracker stop and prices.

Where it comes from: No moods: system light and dark (spirit of the revised H).

Derived tonal steps, each added only because a pair failed with the base hexes:

- **From the revised H, unchanged #ECE6DA, #C5D6BE, #33493B, #4F6B58, #8FAE96, #D49A5E; dark #22402F, #2E2A25, #7A5A3E, #7FA08E, #4F7360, #292927, #9CCBAE** (from today's hues): I reuses the revised H's light page, its Malam header family (sage) and its Siang meal card family (cream with an apricot edge); no new step was needed.

Active tracker stop (a colour only this direction sets; checked here, outside the 156-pair count, at 3:1 against the meal card): light-siang #9B4309 on #FFF7E9 6.17:1, light-malam #9B4309 on #FFF7E9 6.17:1, dark-siang #F47B2A on #2E2A25 5.23:1, dark-malam #F47B2A on #2E2A25 5.23:1.

Accent on prices (sunrise ink in H and I, copper in G, bold ink in K; text on the plain card; outside the count, at 4.5:1): light-siang #9B4309 on #FFFEFA 6.5:1, light-malam #9B4309 on #FFFEFA 6.5:1, dark-siang #F5C9A6 on #292927 9.57:1, dark-malam #F5C9A6 on #292927 9.57:1.

Header: Sage header in both modes: forest's light tint carries the brand without implying a time of day.

No moods: the script is keyed on four looks, so the Siang and Malam columns below carry the same values. Light and dark are the only real looks; the 156-pair count therefore checks each pair twice per theme. The toggle keys (toggleTrack, toggleActive) stay for the meal-time segmented control, not a mood.

#### What removing the moods touches (G-b scope note; no code changed)

- **Tokens** (`packages/design-tokens/src/index.ts`): `nativeMood`, the `Mood` and `MoodKey` types and `MoodPalette` go or collapse to one palette per theme (header, hero, markers, track) folded into `nativeThemes`. `nativeArt.successDisc` reads `nativeMood.light.siang.header` and needs its own fixed value.
- **Mood plumbing** (`packages/mobile-ui/src`): `mood.tsx` (`MoodProvider`, `useMood`, `useMoodColors`, `defaultMood`, `MoodLabels`), `MoodHeader`, `MoodFill`, `brand/MoodArc`, `brand/useMoodProgress`, `brand/MalamPattern`, plus mood reads in `components.tsx`, `RantangTrack`, `StatusBand`, `DemoStrip`, `Skeleton`, `CalendarPhotoCell`, `TopInset`, `screenInsets` and `toastAnchor`.
- **Customer app** (13 files read mood, for example `today/Beranda.tsx`, `today/Plate.tsx`, `discover/Jelajah.tsx`, `schedule/Jadwal.tsx`, `plan/PlanDetail.tsx`): the sun/moon `MoodArc` switch on Beranda goes. Lunch and dinner still exist as meals, so Beranda shows them as one card per meal or a plain Makan siang / Makan malam segmented control. Jelajah's Siang/Malam track becomes a meal-time filter with no colour change.
- **Dapur** (7 files, for example `today/TodayScreen.tsx`, `RoleGate.tsx`): the Siang/Malam toggle on Hari ini also picks the meal session (one session per meal), so it stays as a plain Makan siang / Makan malam segmented control with no colour change.
- **Artwork**: the mascot and food art are the same files in every mood, so nothing is recoloured. Only the Malam pattern and the success disc's header-derived colour are affected.
- **Persisted preference**: there is none to migrate. The mood lives in memory, one provider per app, and each launch defaults from the Jakarta clock (Siang before 15:00, `defaultMood` in `mood.tsx`). The theme preference ("Ikuti sistem") is separate and stays.
- **Tests**: `tests/native-contrast.test.ts` (mood rows) and `tests/native-theme-guard.test.ts` read mood, as do any app tests of the arc or toggle; the four-look checks become two-look checks. DESIGN.md's mood table is superseded.

### J · C, with a green ground and brown highlights

Terang Siang and Terang Malam are C unchanged. In dark mode the page is not charcoal: it is C's Malam forest, one step deeper, with cards, tab bar and the meal card in lighter steps of the same green. What was green in C's dark looks is brown: light brown in Gelap Siang (a tan header, a caramel button) and dark brown in Gelap Malam (a kopi header, a dark brown button with a latte edge). Card edges, markers and the selected tab follow the mood's brown.

Where it comes from: C, with green-ground dark mode and brown highlights (owner, 2026-10-13) (light looks are C; dark looks rebuilt on C's Terang Malam forest).

Base colours: Forest (C Terang Malam header) #163D2E, Forest deep (dark page) #0F2C21, Light brown (Gelap Siang) #C9A57D, Caramel (Gelap Siang button) #DDB283, Dark brown (Gelap Malam) #6B4A2B.

Derived tonal steps, each added only because a pair failed with the base hexes:

- **Forest deep (dark page) #0F2C21** (from C's Terang Malam forest #163D2E): the dark page: forest cards on a forest page would be 1.0:1; one step deeper puts #163D2E cards and the tab bar 1.24:1 off it and the kopi Malam header 1.29:1.
- **Forest lift (meal card) #24533F** (from Forest #163D2E): the dark meal card: 1.70:1 off the page and 1.32:1 off the kopi Malam header, so the tan and kopi headers both part from it without an edge.
- **Muted on forest #D3CEC4** (from C's dark muted #B5B2AA): muted text on green: #B5B2AA is under 4.5:1 on #163D2E; this step is 7.69:1 on cards and 5.62:1 on the meal card.
- **Latte (text-safe brown) #E6C3A2** (from the light brown #C9A57D): the selected tab label and headings on green (7.29:1 on the tab bar), the Malam header meta, and the edge that carries the dark brown button (5.32:1 on the meal card; the dark brown fill alone is 1.11:1 there).
- **Kopi header (Gelap Malam) #4A3423** (from dark brown #6B4A2B): a dark brown header that parts from the page (1.29:1) and the meal card (1.32:1); #6B4A2B itself is only 1.11:1 on the meal card. Also the Gelap Siang header meta, selected chip, active marker and today ring on the tan header (5.07:1).
- **Brown light (Malam idle marker) #B08A66** (from dark brown #6B4A2B): idle marker and track edge on the kopi header at 3:1 or more; the dark brown itself would vanish there.
- **Oat (Gelap Siang track and search) #F1E1CC** (from light brown #C9A57D): the meal-time track and search field on the tan header, so the deep green header text reads on them (idle tab label at 4.5:1 or more).
- **Brown mid (Gelap Siang edges) #A9845C** (from light brown #C9A57D): card and tab bar edges in Gelap Siang (4.38:1 on the page) and the decorative arc track.
- **Kopi track and pattern #5C4129 / #553C28 / #7A5A3E** (from dark brown #6B4A2B): the Gelap Malam meal-time track, Malam pattern and arc track, replacing C's greens #2C5A45, #2A5743 and #3A6652.

### K · Today's palette, used with restraint

Today's forest, sunrise, cream and charcoal, spent the way top food apps spend a brand colour. Page, header and tab bar are cream and neutrals (charcoal in dark); the meal card is a plain card that stands off the header by a fine edge, so the food photo carries the warmth. Forest fills only the primary button and marks the active tab, the selected segment and today's date. The tracker is ink; sunrise marks only the current stop. Prices are bold ink, and Gagal diantar keeps its own red with its words. Siang and Malam are a faint header tint and the sun/moon mark, nothing more.

Where it comes from: Today's palette, used with restraint (Mobbin rules, owner 2026-10-13) (palette/mobbin-colour-restraint.md).

Base colours: Forest #163D2E, Sunrise #F47B2A, Sunrise ink #9B4309, Cream #FFF7E9, Charcoal #2E2E2E.

Derived tonal steps, each added only because a pair failed with the base hexes:

- **Page neutral #E4DED1** (from today's canvas #FDFAF3 / cream): the light page one step down, so white cards (1.33:1), the cream tab bar and Siang header (1.26:1) and the pale sage Malam header (1.23:1) stand off it without a border; today's canvas is 1.03:1 under white cards.
- **Pale sage header #F3F6EF** (from today's sage #F0F3E9 (forest tint)): the faint Malam tint: #F0F3E9 is 1.19:1 on the page; this step is 1.23:1 (also the revised H's Malam card).
- **Muted deep #555B54** (from today's muted #60675F): muted text, header meta, demo strip and placeholder: #60675F is 4.35:1 on the page; this step is 5.2:1 or more on every light ground.
- **Card edge #C9C6BA** (from today's line #E2E3D8): the meal card's edge: the white card is 1.06:1 on the cream header and 1.08:1 on the pale sage one; the edge is 1.61:1 and 1.57:1.
- **Lines (decorative) #D3CEC1 / #3A3A37** (from today's line): row separators re-stepped for the page tone; decorative, not in the pair list.
- **Dark card #292927** (from today's dark surface #232321): dark cards, tab bar, meal card and the selected segment fill: #232321 is 1.16:1 on the dark page; this step is 1.25:1.
- **Dark warm header #2E2B25** (from today's dark sage #2A2D27, warmed toward cream): the faint Gelap Siang tint, the dark twin of the cream header (Gelap Malam keeps today's dark sage #2A2D27).
- **Dark card edge #4E4C47** (from today's dark line #34332F): the dark meal card's edge: the card is 1.03:1 on either dark header; the edge is 1.65:1 and 1.63:1.
- **Forest light #9CCBAE** (from Forest #163D2E): the dark primary button, today ring, active tab label and selected segment ring: forest itself is 1.21:1 on the dark card.
- **Overlay ink #1B1B1A** (from Charcoal #2E2E2E): a neutral photo overlay and failed-photo ground, so the photo text block carries no brand hue.

Active tracker stop (a colour only this direction sets; checked here, outside the 156-pair count, at 3:1 against the meal card): light-siang #9B4309 on #FFFEFA 6.5:1, light-malam #9B4309 on #FFFEFA 6.5:1, dark-siang #F47B2A on #292927 5.35:1, dark-malam #F47B2A on #292927 5.35:1.

Accent on prices (sunrise ink in H and I, copper in G, bold ink in K; text on the plain card; outside the count, at 4.5:1): light-siang #2E2E2E on #FFFEFA 13.46:1, light-malam #2E2E2E on #FFFEFA 13.46:1, dark-siang #F5F1E8 on #292927 12.93:1, dark-malam #F5F1E8 on #292927 12.93:1.

Branded moment: One branded moment per screen, and only where it means something: Paket selesai and payment success. The mascot sits on the success disc (nativeArt.successDisc, pinned to #FFEFD9, with its 3dp sunrise ink ring) above the one forest button. Drawn as the seventh phone in the grid below. Everywhere else the character comes from the food photos, the mascot art and the type.

### G (v1) · Linen, olive and forest

Built from your reference palette. Light is linen and beige with forest ink: beige cards and tab bar one tonal step below a linen page, a linen meal card with a taupe edge, a beige Siang header and a forest Malam header, and a forest button. Dark is made from forest and taupe depths, not grey. There is no orange: warnings and markers use the palette's own deep tones.

Where it comes from: Owner's reference palette (five colours supplied on 2026-10-13).

Base colours: Linen Cream #EDE5D8, Warm Beige #D8C8B4, Mushroom Taupe #9B8D7A, Olive Sage #6E7C61, Forest Green #2F4A3A.

Derived tonal steps, each added only because a pair failed with the base hexes:

- **Taupe deep #5A4F42** (from Mushroom Taupe #9B8D7A): muted text, header meta, today ring and the light Gagal diantar stop: taupe as muted text is 2.59:1 on linen and 1.98:1 on beige; this step gives 6.38:1 on linen and 4.88:1 on beige.
- **Olive deep #5E6B52** (from Olive Sage #6E7C61): idle marker, track and search edge on the beige Siang header (olive is 2.72:1 there, this step 3.47:1), and the light demo strip fill (linen text on olive is 3.56:1, on this step 4.54:1; white on olive is 4.45:1).
- **Taupe light #B0A390** (from Mushroom Taupe #9B8D7A): idle marker and track edge on the forest Malam header: taupe is 3.00:1 there, at the 3:1 floor with no margin; this step gives 3.93:1.
- **Forest night #16231C** (from Forest Green #2F4A3A): dark canvas, Malam track, dark search field and the photo ground: forest cards on a forest page would be 1.0:1; this step puts forest cards 1.67:1 off the page.
- **Taupe night #3E362D** (from Mushroom Taupe #9B8D7A): dark Siang header and dark Malam meal card: needs a dark ground that is not forest, so the header and the forest meal card (or the forest Malam header and the meal card) part at 1.2:1 or more.

### H (v1) · Today's palette, neutral-led

Today's hues, with neutrals doing most of the work. Headers are a tinted neutral: warm cream for Siang, cool sage for Malam, the same two tones darkened in dark mode. The meal card is the plain card with a fine edge, the page one neutral step down, markers charcoal and grey. Forest is spent on the main button and the today ring; sunrise ink marks the one active tracker stop.

Where it comes from: Today's palette, neutral-led (forest, sunrise, cream, charcoal and today's neutrals).

Derived tonal steps, each added only because a pair failed with the base hexes:

- **Page neutral #E0DBCE** (from today's canvas #FDFAF3 / cream): the light page: today's canvas sits 1.03:1 under white cards and 1.08:1 under the cream header; this step puts cards 1.37:1, the cream (Siang) header 1.30:1 and the sage (Malam) header 1.23:1 off the page.
- **Muted deep #555B54** (from today's muted #60675F): muted text, header meta, demo strip text: #60675F is 4.22:1 on the new page; this step gives 5.05:1 or more on every light ground.
- **Card edge #C9C6BA** (from today's line #E2E3D8): the meal card's drawn edge (light): the white card is 1.06:1 on the cream header and 1.11:1 on the sage one; the edge reaches 1.61:1 and 1.52:1 (also the light arc track, decorative).
- **Dark card #292927** (from today's dark surface #232321): dark cards, tab bar and meal card: #232321 is 1.16:1 on the dark page; this step clears 1.2:1.
- **Dark warm header #2E2B25** (from today's dark sage #2A2D27, warmed toward cream): dark Siang header, the dark twin of the cream header (Malam keeps today's dark sage #2A2D27).
- **Dark card edge #4E4C47** (from today's dark line #34332F): the dark meal card's edge: the card is about 1.04:1 on either dark header; the edge reaches 1.63:1 or more.
- **Forest light #9CCBAE** (from Forest #163D2E): the dark primary button, today ring and selected tab label: forest itself is 1.21:1 on the dark card; the same hue lightened keeps forest's role in dark mode.
- **Overlay ink #1B1B1A** (from Charcoal #2E2E2E): a neutral photo overlay instead of today's green-black one, so the photo text block carries no hue.
- **Lines (decorative) #D3CEC1 / #3A3A37** (from today's line): row separators re-stepped for the new page tone; decorative, not in the pair list.

Active tracker stop (a colour only this direction sets; checked here, outside the 156-pair count, at 3:1 against the meal card): light-siang #9B4309 on #FFFEFA 6.5:1, light-malam #9B4309 on #FFFEFA 6.5:1, dark-siang #F47B2A on #292927 5.35:1, dark-malam #F47B2A on #292927 5.35:1.
